HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001205/B006","root_001392/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_0bf1e09e183459b1c49a","text":"3. **A hidden gestation before dawn.** Lexical evidence: `ل ي ل` B001, darkness; `ق د ر` B006, fitting proportion; activated by the basmalah's `ر ح م` B003, womb, and 97:5's `ف ج ر` B001/B002, splitting/outflow and dawn-breaking. The packet supplies descent into night, womb-branch mercy at the opening, and dawn as a breaking threshold; I supply the causal assumption that the dark measured interval protects formation until release. Reading change: the descent is not just arrival at night but implantation into a measured enclosure whose completion becomes visible at dawn.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001205/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1011149d1d1230c51e24","text":"5. **Effective capacity under authority.** **ق د ر B003** supplies capacity, power, mastery, and effective means. The later descent of angels and Spirit is constrained by permission from their Lord (97:4), so the packet presents both large operative scope and a hierarchy of authorization. I infer that the night's capacity is exercised through, rather than outside, that authority chain. The fixed phrase can therefore denote a night in which effective power becomes operative without implying autonomous power in the descending agents.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001132/B002","root_001392/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_13d036c1c4e9282b9312","text":"2. **A bounded dark chamber of operation.** **ل ي ل B001** supplies night, its darkness, and its opposition to day. The fixed prepositional enclosure *فِى لَيْلَةِ* is resumed by *فِيهَا* in 97:4 and clipped by *حَتَّىٰ مَطْلَعِ ٱلْفَجْرِ* in 97:5, where **ف ج ر B002** is dawn breaking from night. I infer that the darkness contains and temporarily veils the descent and its agents. *Night* therefore becomes an operational chamber with an entry, interior activity, and exit boundary, rather than a mere date.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000051/B001","root_001205/B005","root_001205/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_149d2ff9615873818aea","text":"4. **Deliberate ordering.** **ق د ر B005** supplies arranging, estimating, planning, and proportioning by deliberation; **ق د ر B006** adds fitting proportion. In 97:4 the agents descend under permission *مِّن كُلِّ أَمْرٍ*, where **ء م ر B001/B002** permits both affair and directive. I infer that authorized treatment of every affair is the operational manifestation of the fixed ayah's *qadr*. The phrase then reads as a night of calibrated ordering, not only a night possessing an unspecified rank.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001392/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_15ed73bdb4637a27741b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"97:1:4\",\"root_001392\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Gece, indirilen şeyi geçici olarak örten, içinde sonraki inişlerin gerçekleştiği ve şafakta açılan sınırlı bir çalışma alanına dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001492/B001","root_001492/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_24b5632af28114cda1e5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"97:1:2\",\"root_001492\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Buradaki tamamlanmış gönderme, daha sonra meleklerin ve Ruh'un sürdürdüğü inişlerin kurucu ilk teslimi olarak belirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_30081adf709118296e63","text":"97:4's later *تَنَزَّلُ* was the main surprise because its changed form, aspect, and subjects corrected a one-movement reading into a founding delivery followed by active deployment. 97:3 and 97:5 together sharpened *qadr* into narrow temporal concentration, while 97:4 made its capacity and arrangement concrete. The later sequence also never names the fixed pronoun's antecedent inside the packet; instead, comparison, agents, peace, and dawn disclose it through effects, strengthening the functional-disclosure reading.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001205/B005","root_001205/B006","root_001492/B002","root_001492/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3b5373d72a342b8df8fa","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"97:1:2\",\"root_001492\",[\"B002\",\"B004\"]],[\"97:1:5\",\"root_001205\",[\"B005\",\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İndirilen şey yalnız aşağıya taşınmaz; her işin izinli biçimde ele alındığı ölçülü ve uygun bir düzene yerleştirilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001492/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-8","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3c7ca2d188278a4ebd1b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"97:1:2\",\"root_001492\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"İndirme, gece halkına önceden hazırlanmış bir ikramın sunulması gibi, yalnız bildirim değil fayda sağlayan bir ağırlama anlamı da kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001492/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_45aa9d0285a1be2c224c","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"97:1:2\",\"root_001492\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Açıkça adlandırılmayan 'o', ne olduğundan çok inişinin doğurduğu karşılaştırma, hareket, esenlik ve şafak sonuçlarıyla tanınır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001492/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:6","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_6d6e0a7c8a414ef19f74","text":"6. **Disclosure through effects rather than immediate naming.** The fixed **ن ز ل B002** governs the unexpanded object pronoun *ـهُ*, while **ل ي ل B001** supplies a concealing dark interval; 97:2 then asks what could make the addressee know the night, and 97:3-5 answer by comparison, activity, and endpoint. The packet never supplies an explicit noun antecedent inside this surah packet. I infer that the withheld object is made legible here through what its descent causes around it. This changes the sentence from simple identification into an event whose content is disclosed functionally.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001205/B006","root_001392/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-7","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_7dccd6d6dad753db3646","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"97:1:4\",\"root_001392\",[\"B001\"]],[\"97:1:5\",\"root_001205\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Karanlık ve ölçülü gece, indirilen şeyin şafakta görünür olacak biçimde oluştuğu koruyucu bir gebelik alanı gibi düşünülebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001205/B001","root_001205/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_8486a257edb1d5874a24","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"97:1:5\",\"root_001205\",[\"B001\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kadir Gecesi, bin aya yayılabilecek değeri dar ve ölçülü bir zaman aralığında yoğunlaştırır; kısalığı güçsüzlük değil yoğunluk bildirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001205/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_84e2e9d28eca1e09ab98","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"97:1:5\",\"root_001205\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Gecenin etkili gücü, meleklerin ve Ruh'un Rablerinin izniyle inişinde işler; bu güç yetki düzeninden bağımsız değildir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001492/B002","root_001492/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-9","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_8beab282acfc58b4cec7","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"97:1:2\",\"root_001492\",[\"B002\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Bu indirme, şafağın erişim noktasına kadar bozulmadan süren yetkili bir teslim zincirinin ilk halkası olarak da okunur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000022/B004","root_001205/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a422f7deb87d9dbb39f7","text":"97:4 retroactively corrected the first impression of \"أَنزَلْنَاهُ\": the later repeated-form \"تَنَزَّلُ\" plus `ء ذ ن` B004, `ر ب ب` B001/B002, and `ء م ر` B001/B002 made descent look like continuing authorized placement, not only a completed past act. 97:5 then added a boundary mechanism: `س ل م` B012 and `ط ل ع` B001/B006 made the descent appear safely transferred until an access/rising point. 97:3's thousand-month comparison also surprised by activating `ق د ر` B004: the night's smallness is not weakness but compressed measure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000452/B005","root_001492/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_b059c976e3ff1623fadb","text":"4. **Hospitality/provision latent in descent.** Lexical evidence: `ن ز ل` B005, guest-provision prepared for a lodger. Activated by `خ ي ر` B005 in 97:3, beneficence and gift, and `س ل م` B001/B004 in 97:5, safety and peace. The packet supplies a sent-down object, superior good, and peace; I supply the hospitable mechanism that what descends is prepared reception/provision for those under the night. Reading change: \"أَنزَلْنَاهُ\" can read as bestowal of prepared sustenance, not only transmission.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000022/B004","root_001205/B005","root_001492/B002","root_001492/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_cfd1a1f49bc043e28738","text":"1. **Descent as ordered placement, not only downward motion.** Lexical evidence: `ن ز ل` B002, causing something to descend, and B004, placing in proper order; `ق د ر` B005, arranging by estimate and deliberation. Activated by 97:4's \"بِإِذْنِ رَبِّهِم مِّن كُلِّ أَمْرٍ\" with `ء ذ ن` B004, `ر ب ب` B001, `ك ل ل` B003, and `ء م ر` B001/B002. The packet supplies \"أَنزَلْنَاهُ\" and the later authorized \"every affair/command\"; I supply the arrow that the thing sent down is placed into a measured administrative order. Reading change: the ayah shifts from a simple event-report into an account of calibrated placement within a commanded system.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001205/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e3d5284fa4c68083fb9d","text":"3. **Measured concentration.** **ق د ر B001** is measure, extent, limit, or term, while **ق د ر B004** is narrowing to a scant measure. One night is set against a thousand months in 97:3 and is given a finite endpoint at dawn in 97:5. The packet does not state a compression formula; I infer from the extreme one-to-thousand comparison that value or efficacy normally spread across a vast duration is concentrated into the narrow interval. *لَيْلَةِ ٱلْقَدْرِ* can thus read as the night of bounded measure and temporal concentration at once.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000045/B001","root_001205/B001","root_001205/B004","root_001392/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ed9984c5747c214c1c8b","text":"2. **Night as a concentrated vessel of measure.** Lexical evidence: `ل ي ل` B001, night and its darkness; `ق د ر` B001, measured extent/limit, and B004, narrowing to a scant measure. Activated by 97:3's comparison with `ء ل ف` B001, thousand, and `ش ه ر` B001, month made known by the crescent. The packet supplies one night against a thousand months; I supply the compression arrow: a limited dark unit can contain what would otherwise be spread across vast counted duration. Reading change: \"لَيْلَةِ ٱلْقَدْرِ\" reads as a temporal compression chamber, not merely a calendar label.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001492/B001","root_001492/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f023cd56a6eeea508393","text":"1. **A founding delivery followed by distributed descent.** In the fixed ayah, causative *أَنزَلْنَٰهُ* activates **ن ز ل B002**, bringing or sending something down; 97:4's *تَنَزَّلُ* activates **ن ز ل B001**, descent or gradual alighting. The perfect causative with a singular object precedes the later imperfect-form descent of plural agents. The packet supplies that sequence; I infer that the first delivery establishes the event that the later descents attend or implement. The ayah consequently reads as the founding movement in a staged delivery system, not as an isolated downward motion.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
